2013

2,275 council actions · 0 linked to the city's scans · 8 sections

← 2012 · all years · 2014 →

The clerk's own numbering, from the Final Actions document published after each 2013 meeting. [?] marks an uncertain reading; ⤳ marks a numbering jump. scan opens the city's PDF. Where the document itself has been read, name is its main sponsor and 9 the members voting in favour. A voice vote records only that count; where the council took a roll call the record keeps both sides, 6–3, and where a measure lost, 1–7. Neither ever records who cast which vote.
